\section{Purpose and workflow}\label{purpose-and-workflow}

This document is a self-contained \textbf{R-first, QGIS-processed}
workflow for Tharaka Sub-county. It does not require Google Earth
Engine, \texttt{rgee}, \texttt{reticulate}, Google Drive exports, or
Google Maps. It queries public Landsat Collection 2 Level-2 imagery from
the Microsoft Planetary Computer STAC catalogue, creates locally cached
seasonal composites, applies QGIS Processing through
\texttt{qgisprocess}, and produces all maps, raster outputs, tables,
diagnostics, and trend plots in a results folder beside this R Markdown
file.

The four target years are \textbf{2000, 2010, 2020 and 2025}. The four
requested observation windows are February 10--20, September 15--25, May
5--30 and December 5--30. Landsat's repeat cycle means that a usable
acquisition may not fall inside every narrow window. When necessary, the
retrieval code searches a wider date interval, records the actual
acquisition dates, and selects a small number of scenes for a
cloud-masked composite. The report must be read together with
\texttt{Tharaka\_Seasonal\_Scene\_Inventory.csv} to see the dates
actually used.

\textbf{Resolution:} source imagery and the final raster analysis use a
common 30 m grid in EPSG:6933. The map-rendering steps downsample only
for display; the stored composite and classified GeoTIFFs remain 30 m.
The primary method is pooled, image-led unsupervised clustering: it
finds recurring spectral/seasonal groups but does not automatically
prove which group is forest, bushland, grassland, farmland or
settlement. A reviewable cluster crosswalk is generated. Named
land-cover trends are only produced after that crosswalk is completed
and the document is rerun.

\subsection{Compatibility fix in this
version}\label{compatibility-fix-in-this-version}

This version contains a defensive raster-stack helper. It explicitly
converts \texttt{terra::nlyr()} results to integer values inside
\texttt{vapply()} and verifies that stacked layers are
\texttt{SpatRaster} objects before reprojection. This addresses both the
\texttt{vapply()} integer/double mismatch and the prior
\texttt{terra::project(x\ =\ "list")} failure.

\subsection{Files generated}\label{files-generated}

The document creates \texttt{01\_data/} beside itself for inputs and
cached rasters, and \texttt{Tharaka\_Land\_Cover\_Results/} beside
itself for final results. It reuses valid cached outputs on reruns; set
\texttt{REBUILD\_SEASONAL\_COMPOSITES\ \textless{}-\ TRUE} in the
configuration chunk to rebuild composites. Internet access is needed the
first time missing historical scenes are retrieved. A working QGIS
installation is required for the QGIS Processing steps.

\section{2. Load or obtain the study
boundary}\label{load-or-obtain-the-study-boundary}

The document first looks for the boundary used in the existing project.
If it is not found, it downloads GADM level-2 Kenya boundaries and
selects Tharaka in Tharaka-Nithi County. The selected polygon is
validated and saved to the project so later runs do not need to download
it again.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{local\_boundary\_candidates }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
  \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_SubCounty.gpkg"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_SubCounty.shp"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"qgisprocess"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_SubCounty\_QGIS\_fixed.gpkg"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{local\_boundary\_candidates }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ local\_boundary\_candidates[}\FunctionTok{file.exists}\NormalTok{(local\_boundary\_candidates)]}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(local\_boundary\_candidates)) \{}
\NormalTok{  boundary\_source\_path }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ local\_boundary\_candidates[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{  boundary\_source\_sf }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_read}\NormalTok{(boundary\_source\_path, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  boundary\_source\_name }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Existing project boundary: "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{basename}\NormalTok{(boundary\_source\_path))}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
  \FunctionTok{message}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"No local Tharaka boundary found; obtaining GADM level{-}2 boundaries."}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  gadm\_file\_guess }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"gadm41\_KEN\_2.rds"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  gadm2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ geodata}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{gadm}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{country =} \StringTok{"KEN"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{level =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{path =}\NormalTok{ boundary\_dir)}
\NormalTok{  gadm2\_sf }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_as\_sf}\NormalTok{(gadm2)}
\NormalTok{  required\_name\_fields }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"NAME\_1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"NAME\_2"}\NormalTok{)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(required\_name\_fields }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(gadm2\_sf))) \{}
    \FunctionTok{stop}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"The downloaded GADM data did not contain NAME\_1 and NAME\_2. Inspect the boundary attributes."}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \}}
\NormalTok{  match\_idx }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(gadm2\_sf}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{NAME\_1) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"tharaka{-}nithi"} \SpecialCharTok{\&} \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(gadm2\_sf}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{NAME\_2) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"tharaka"}\NormalTok{)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(match\_idx) }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{1}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{    tharaka\_options }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(gadm2\_sf}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{NAME\_2[}\FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"tharaka"}\NormalTok{, gadm2\_sf}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{NAME\_2, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)])}
    \FunctionTok{stop}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Could not identify exactly one GADM feature for Tharaka in Tharaka{-}Nithi. Names containing \textquotesingle{}Tharaka\textquotesingle{}: "}\NormalTok{,}
         \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(tharaka\_options, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{),}
         \StringTok{". Put the reviewed boundary at 01\_data/boundary/Tharaka\_SubCounty.gpkg and rerun."}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \}}
\NormalTok{  boundary\_source\_sf }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ gadm2\_sf[match\_idx, , drop }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{  boundary\_source\_name }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"GADM level{-}2 boundary selected by NAME\_1=Tharaka{-}Nithi, NAME\_2=Tharaka"}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{boundary\_source\_sf }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_make\_valid}\NormalTok{(boundary\_source\_sf)}
\NormalTok{boundary\_source\_sf }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_transform}\NormalTok{(boundary\_source\_sf, }\DecValTok{4326}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{boundary\_union }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_union}\NormalTok{(sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_geometry}\NormalTok{(boundary\_source\_sf))}
\NormalTok{study\_area\_ll }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_sf}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{area\_name =} \StringTok{"Tharaka Sub{-}county"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{geometry =}\NormalTok{ boundary\_union)}
\NormalTok{study\_area\_map }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_transform}\NormalTok{(study\_area\_ll, TARGET\_CRS)}
\NormalTok{study\_area\_map }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_make\_valid}\NormalTok{(study\_area\_map)}
\NormalTok{study\_area\_ha }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_area}\NormalTok{(study\_area\_map)) }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{10000}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{boundary\_wgs84\_file }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_SubCounty\_Source\_WGS84.gpkg"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{boundary\_projected\_file }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_SubCounty\_EPSG6933.gpkg"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{suppressWarnings}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{try}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unlink}\NormalTok{(boundary\_wgs84\_file), }\AttributeTok{silent =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\FunctionTok{suppressWarnings}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{try}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unlink}\NormalTok{(boundary\_projected\_file), }\AttributeTok{silent =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_write}\NormalTok{(study\_area\_ll, boundary\_wgs84\_file, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{delete\_dsn =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_write}\NormalTok{(study\_area\_map, boundary\_projected\_file, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{delete\_dsn =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Repair the projected study boundary using QGIS Processing. This is an actual}
\CommentTok{\# qgisprocess operation, not a call to Earth Engine or a map{-}tile service.}
\NormalTok{qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(boundary\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_SubCounty\_QGIS\_Fixed\_EPSG6933.gpkg"}\NormalTok{)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{file.exists}\NormalTok{(qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file)) }\FunctionTok{unlink}\NormalTok{(qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file)}
\NormalTok{qgis\_boundary\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ qgisprocess}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{qgis\_run\_algorithm}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{algorithm =} \StringTok{"native:fixgeometries"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{INPUT =}\NormalTok{ boundary\_projected\_file,}
  \AttributeTok{OUTPUT =}\NormalTok{ qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file,}
  \AttributeTok{.quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{file.exists}\NormalTok{(qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file)) \{}
  \FunctionTok{stop}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"QGIS geometry repair did not create the expected output: "}\NormalTok{, qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\NormalTok{qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_sf }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_read}\NormalTok{(qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_file, }\AttributeTok{quiet =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(sf}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{st\_is\_valid}\NormalTok{(qgis\_fixed\_boundary\_sf))) }\FunctionTok{stop}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"QGIS boundary repair completed, but invalid geometry remains."}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{boundary\_info }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ tibble}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{tibble}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{boundary\_source =}\NormalTok{ boundary\_source\_name,}
  \AttributeTok{source\_path =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{exists}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"boundary\_source\_path"}\NormalTok{)) boundary\_source\_path }\ControlFlowTok{else} \StringTok{"GADM download"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{crs\_for\_analysis =}\NormalTok{ TARGET\_CRS,}
  \AttributeTok{area\_ha =}\NormalTok{ study\_area\_ha,}
  \AttributeTok{area\_km2 =}\NormalTok{ study\_area\_ha }\SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{qgis\_version =}\NormalTok{ qgis\_version\_used}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{readr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(boundary\_info, }\FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(tables\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_Study\_Area\_Metadata.csv"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{knitr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(boundary\_info, }\AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{caption =} \StringTok{"Study{-}area and processing metadata"}\NormalTok{)}

\section{3. Create the common 30 m target
grid}\label{create-the-common-30-m-target-grid}

EPSG:6933 is used to provide an equal-area, 30 m grid across the study
area, which crosses the equator. The grid covers the boundary envelope
plus a one-cell margin; QGIS clips the resulting composites to the
actual polygon.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\NormalTok{res\_m }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ TARGET\_RESOLUTION\_M}
\NormalTok{x\_min }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{floor}\NormalTok{(terra}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{xmin}\NormalTok{(bb) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ res\_m) }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ res\_m }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ res\_m}
\NormalTok{x\_max }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ceiling}\NormalTok{(terra}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{xmax}\NormalTok{(bb) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ res\_m) }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ res\_m }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ res\_m}

\NormalTok{ncols }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((x\_max }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ x\_min) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ res\_m))}
\NormalTok{nrows }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.integer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((y\_max }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ y\_min) }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ res\_m))}

\section{4. Discover, cache and composite public Landsat
imagery}\label{discover-cache-and-composite-public-landsat-imagery}

Source:
\href{https://planetarycomputer.microsoft.com/dataset/landsat-c2-l2}{Microsoft
Planetary Computer Landsat Collection 2 Level-2 STAC collection}. The
workflow reads the blue, green, red, near-infrared, SWIR1 and SWIR2
surface-reflectance assets plus QA assets. Reflectance scaling follows
Landsat Collection 2 Level-2 conventions
(\texttt{DN\ ×\ 0.0000275\ −\ 0.2}). \texttt{QA\_PIXEL} masks fill,
dilated cloud, cirrus, cloud, cloud shadow and snow. Pixels flagged by
\texttt{QA\_RADSAT} are also masked conservatively.

The processing stages are cached: individual cropped/reprojected scenes
are saved once, then combined into median seasonal composites.
Subsequent runs use existing valid composites and do not repeat the
catalogue search or asset reads unless rebuilding is requested.

\section{5. Build seasonal spectral-index layers in
R}\label{build-seasonal-spectral-index-layers-in-r}

The index stack includes NDVI, NDMI, SAVI, EVI, NDWI, MNDWI, NBR and
NDBI, alongside the six reflectance bands. Index formulae are calculated
explicitly from the harmonized six-band names. These indices provide
complementary vegetation, moisture, water and built-up-surface signals;
no single index is treated as a definitive land-cover label.

\section{6. Create the 16 seasonal Landsat scene
maps}\label{create-the-16-seasonal-landsat-scene-maps}

All maps use a pooled percentile stretch per spectral band so the
displayed contrast is comparable across years and windows. The primary
individual maps are false-colour NIR--red--green composites, useful for
inspecting vegetation patterns. A true-colour 16-panel contact sheet is
also produced as a visual aid. Map display pixels are aggregated only to
keep plotting efficient; the underlying GeoTIFFs remain at 30 m.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\section{7. Summarize the seasonal vegetation and moisture
signals}\label{summarize-the-seasonal-vegetation-and-moisture-signals}

These are sub-county raster summaries, not pilot-point estimates. The
median and interquartile range are computed from sampled pixels for
every year and seasonal window. The full values are in
\texttt{tables/Tharaka\_Seasonal\_Spectral\_Index\_Summary.csv}.

  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(index }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"NDVI"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"NDMI"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"SAVI"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"NDBI"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\section{8. Fit one pooled unsupervised model across
years}\label{fit-one-pooled-unsupervised-model-across-years}

A pooled model is used so cluster IDs have the same centroid definitions
across 2000, 2010, 2020 and 2025. Each year contributes a sample of
valid 30 m pixels, using all four seasonal windows and their
reflectance/index features. Feature standardization prevents indices and
reflectance variables with different numeric spreads from dominating the
Euclidean distance. The model is exploratory and requires
interpretation/validation.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# 8.1 Sample each year while keeping the feature grid on disk}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{training\_samples }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (yr }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ years) \{}
  \FunctionTok{message}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Sampling complete multiseason feature vectors for "}\NormalTok{, yr)}
\NormalTok{  feat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{feature\_stack\_for\_year}\NormalTok{(yr)}
\NormalTok{  samp }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(terra}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{spatSample}\NormalTok{(feat, }\AttributeTok{size =}\NormalTok{ SAMPLE\_PIXELS\_PER\_YEAR,}
                                     \AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"random"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
                                     \AttributeTok{as.df =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) \{}
    \FunctionTok{message}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Sampling issue in "}\NormalTok{, yr, }\StringTok{": "}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{conditionMessage}\NormalTok{(e)); }\ConstantTok{NULL}
\NormalTok{  \})}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.null}\NormalTok{(samp) }\SpecialCharTok{||} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(samp) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{100}\DataTypeTok{L}\NormalTok{) \{}
    \FunctionTok{stop}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Too few complete pixels were available for year "}\NormalTok{, yr,}
         \StringTok{". Inspect seasonal composites for cloud{-}mask gaps or broaden the date window."}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \}}
\NormalTok{  samp }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(samp)}
\NormalTok{  samp }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ samp[}\FunctionTok{complete.cases}\NormalTok{(samp), , drop }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{  samp}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{year }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ yr}
\NormalTok{  training\_samples[[}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(yr)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ samp}
  \FunctionTok{rm}\NormalTok{(feat, samp); }\FunctionTok{gc}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{verbose =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{9. Plot the image-derived cluster maps and
trends}\label{plot-the-image-derived-cluster-maps-and-trends}

The first run produces cluster IDs (1--8 by default), not verified
land-cover names. Area figures are calculated from the classified
\textbf{30 m equal-area grid}, with the shared valid-pixel mask used for
all four years by default. The trend lines connect four snapshots; they
are not annual estimates.

\section{10. Reviewable cluster-to-land-cover
crosswalk}\label{reviewable-cluster-to-land-cover-crosswalk}

The crosswalk is created only once so that a later rerun does not
overwrite your interpretations. Review the map, index summaries,
seasonal RGB images and cluster-specific spectral profiles, then edit
\texttt{Tharaka\_Land\_Cover\_Results/tables/Tharaka\_Cluster\_to\_LandCover\_Crosswalk.csv}.
Every cluster must be assigned a supported category before named class
trend plots are generated. Use \texttt{other\_uncertain} where the
available evidence does not support a confident assignment.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{crosswalk\_file }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(tables\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_Cluster\_to\_LandCover\_Crosswalk.csv"}\NormalTok{)}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{file.exists}\NormalTok{(crosswalk\_file)) \{}
\NormalTok{  readr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(tibble}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{tibble}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cluster =} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(N\_CLUSTERS), }\AttributeTok{land\_cover =} \FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(}\ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}\NormalTok{, N\_CLUSTERS),}
                                  \AttributeTok{interpretation\_notes =} \FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Review cluster map + spectral signatures; do not infer a name from ID alone."}\NormalTok{, N\_CLUSTERS)),}
\NormalTok{                   crosswalk\_file)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\NormalTok{crosswalk }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ readr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{read\_csv}\NormalTok{(crosswalk\_file, }\AttributeTok{show\_col\_types =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{col\_types =} \FunctionTok{cols}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cluster =} \FunctionTok{col\_integer}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{land\_cover =} \FunctionTok{col\_character}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{interpretation\_notes =} \FunctionTok{col\_character}\NormalTok{()))}
\NormalTok{allowed\_land\_cover }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"forest"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"bushland"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"grassland"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"farmland"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"settlement"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"other\_uncertain"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{crosswalk\_complete }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"cluster"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"land\_cover"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(crosswalk)) }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&}
  \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(crosswalk) }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ N\_CLUSTERS }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&} \FunctionTok{setequal}\NormalTok{(crosswalk}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{cluster, }\FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(N\_CLUSTERS)) }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&}
  \FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(crosswalk}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{land\_cover)) }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&} \FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nzchar}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{trimws}\NormalTok{(crosswalk}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{land\_cover))) }\SpecialCharTok{\&\&}
  \FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{trimws}\NormalTok{(crosswalk}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{land\_cover)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ allowed\_land\_cover)}

\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{crosswalk\_complete) \{}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"**Land{-}cover names not yet assigned.** The image{-}derived cluster area and share plots are valid as cluster trends, but named forest/bushland/grassland/farmland/settlement trends are intentionally withheld.}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Edit this file and rerun the document: \textasciigrave{}"}\NormalTok{, crosswalk\_file, }\StringTok{"\textasciigrave{}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  knitr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(crosswalk, }\AttributeTok{caption =} \StringTok{"Crosswalk waiting for review"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
\NormalTok{  crosswalk }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ crosswalk }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{land\_cover =} \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{trimws}\NormalTok{(land\_cover)))}
\NormalTok{  named\_area }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ cluster\_area }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(crosswalk }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(cluster, land\_cover), }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"cluster"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(year, land\_cover) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{area\_ha =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(area\_ha, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{mapped\_area\_ha =} \FunctionTok{first}\NormalTok{(mapped\_area\_ha), }\AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{share\_percent =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(mapped\_area\_ha }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{100} \SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ area\_ha }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ mapped\_area\_ha, }\ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(land\_cover, year)}
\NormalTok{  readr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(named\_area, }\FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(tables\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_30m\_Named\_LandCover\_Area\_Trends.csv"}\NormalTok{))}

\NormalTok{  p\_named\_area }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(named\_area, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ year, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ area\_ha, }\AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ land\_cover)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"\#315A7D"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{linewidth =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{shape =} \DecValTok{21}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size =} \FloatTok{2.8}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{stroke =} \FloatTok{0.35}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"white"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{"\#E76F51"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{facet\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{land\_cover, }\AttributeTok{scales =} \StringTok{"free\_y"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ncol =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{scale\_x\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{breaks =}\NormalTok{ years) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{scale\_y\_continuous}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{labels =}\NormalTok{ scales}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{label\_number}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{big.mark =} \StringTok{","}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{accuracy =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
    \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Named land{-}cover area trends in Tharaka Sub{-}county"}\NormalTok{,}
         \AttributeTok{subtitle =} \StringTok{"30 m Landsat{-}derived clusters aggregated through the reviewed crosswalk"}\NormalTok{,}
         \AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Target year"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Mapped area (ha)"}\NormalTok{,}

    \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{base\_size =} \FloatTok{11.5}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{plot.title =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{),}

  \FunctionTok{ggsave}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(figures\_dir, }\StringTok{"Tharaka\_30m\_Named\_LandCover\_Area\_Trends.png"}\NormalTok{), p\_named\_area, }\AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \FloatTok{8.5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{dpi =} \DecValTok{320}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{bg =} \StringTok{"white"}\NormalTok{)}

  \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(p\_named\_area)}

\section{11. Diagnostics, methods, and output
inventory}\label{diagnostics-methods-and-output-inventory}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Record key settings, versions, provenance and caveats.}
\NormalTok{package\_versions }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ tibble}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{tibble}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{package =}\NormalTok{ required\_packages,}
  \AttributeTok{version =} \FunctionTok{vapply}\NormalTok{(required\_packages, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(p) }\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(utils}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{packageVersion}\NormalTok{(p)), }\FunctionTok{character}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{readr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{write\_csv}\NormalTok{(package\_versions, }\FunctionTok{file.path}\NormalTok{(diagnostics\_dir, }\StringTok{"R\_Package\_Versions.csv"}\NormalTok{))}

\subsection{Interpretation and
limitations}\label{interpretation-and-limitations}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  These are four temporal snapshots, not a complete annual time series.
  Connecting lines show differences between the four target years only.
\item
  Landsat Collection 2 Level-2 is 30 m, but cross-sensor spectral
  differences remain possible between Landsat 5/7 and Landsat 8/9. The
  pooled model makes cluster IDs consistent; it does not eliminate every
  sensor-related bias.
\item
  A narrow requested window may have no usable overpass. The script
  records scene IDs, actual acquisition dates, scene-level cloud
  metadata and any dates outside the requested range. Check that
  inventory before interpreting seasonal differences.
\item
  Unsupervised clusters are spectral groups, not verified land-cover
  classes. Complete the generated crosswalk and conduct independent
  validation before reporting named-class changes as definitive.
\item
  The QA mask is conservative, and using a common valid-pixel mask
  improves comparability across years but can reduce the analysed area.
  Consult \texttt{diagnostics/Tharaka\_Valid\_Pixel\_Coverage.csv}
  before interpreting trends.
\item
  This workflow intentionally avoids MODIS 500 m trends as the primary
  result. Any figure described as 30 m derives from the local Landsat
  GeoTIFFs and the common 30 m analysis grid.
\end{enumerate}
